function getTimeFromDate(a){if(a==null)return 0;var b=(""+a).split(" "),a=b[0].split("-"),b=b.length==1?[0,0,0]:b[1].split(":");return(new Date(parseInt(a[0],10),parseInt(a[1],10)-1,parseInt(a[2],10),parseInt(b[0],10),parseInt(b[1],10),parseInt(b[2],10),0)).getTime()}function convertDateToServerTime(a){var a=a||new Date,b=Math.round(getTimeFromDate(getFormatDate(a))/1E3)+time_server_client_diff;console.log("Time: "+b);a=new Date;a.setTime(b*1E3);return getFormatDate(a)}
function getFormatDate(a){return a.getFullYear()+"-"+padNmb(a.getMonth()+1,2,"0")+"-"+padNmb(a.getDate(),2,"0")+" "+padNmb(a.getHours(),2,"0")+":"+padNmb(a.getMinutes(),2,"0")+":"+padNmb(a.getSeconds(),2,"0")}
function getIntelligentDate(a){a||internationalize("date_error",{capitalize:true});var b=["60","60","24","7","4.35","12","10"],a=Math.round(getTimeFromDate(a)/1E3)+time_server_client_diff,c=Math.round((new Date).getTime()/1E3),d,e;if(c==a&&parseInt(app_abs_dates_format)>0)return internationalize("now",{capitalize:true});if(c>=a){c=c-a;d=internationalize("ago_infosmosis");e=internationalize("hace_infosmosis",{capitalize:true})}else{c=a-c;d=internationalize("from_now_infosmosis");e=internationalize("dentro_de_infosmosis",
{capitalize:true})}c<0&&(c=c*-1);if(parseInt(app_abs_dates_format)>=0&&c>=parseInt(app_abs_dates_format)){b=new Date;b.setTime(a*1E3);a=[internationalize("january",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("february",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("march",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("april",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("may",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("june",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("july",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("august",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("september",
{capitalize:true}),internationalize("october",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("november",{capitalize:true}),internationalize("december",{capitalize:true})];return b.getDate()+" "+internationalize("of")+" "+a[b.getMonth()]+" "+internationalize("of")+" "+b.getFullYear()}for(a=0;c>=b[a]&&a<b.length-1;a++)c=c/b[a];c=Math.round(c);b="";switch(a){case 0:b=c!=1?internationalize("seconds"):internationalize("second");break;case 1:b=c!=1?internationalize("minutes"):internationalize("minute");break;case 2:b=
c!=1?internationalize("hours"):internationalize("hour");break;case 3:b=c!=1?internationalize("days"):internationalize("day");break;case 4:b=c!=1?internationalize("weeks"):internationalize("week");break;case 5:b=c!=1?internationalize("months"):internationalize("month");break;case 6:b=c!=1?internationalize("years"):internationalize("year");break;case 7:b=c!=1?internationalize("decades"):internationalize("decade")}return e+" "+c+" "+b+" "+d}

break;case 8:c=internationalize("august",{capitalize:true});break;case 9:c=internationalize("september",{capitalize:true});break;case 10:c=internationalize("october",{capitalize:true});break;case 11:c=internationalize("november",{capitalize:true});break;case 12:c=internationalize("december",{capitalize:true})}switch(app_user_lang){case "es":return"El "+b[2]+" de "+c+" de "+b[0]+" a las "+a[1];default:return c+" "+b[2]+" "+internationalize("at")+" "+a[1]}}

                            <label for="password">Password</label>
                        </fieldset>
                        <fieldset class="float-label">
                            <input name="repeat-password" autocomplete="repeat-password" type="password" class="form-control" required="">
                            <label for="repeat-password">Repeat password</label>
                        </fieldset>
                    </div>
                    <div class="action-wrapper">
                        <button type="button" class="btn-send">SUBMIT</button>

<script>
    $(function(){
        GiAPI.load(["core"], function(){
            var wrapper = $('.form-wrapper');
            
            wrapper.find('input[name="password"],input[name="repeat-password"]').on("cut copy paste",function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
            });
            
            wrapper.find('.btn-send').click(function(){
                const $p1 = wrapper.find('input[name="password"]');
                const $p2 = wrapper.find('input[name="repeat-password"]');
                
                
                let data = {
                    'h'  : '6816810a8f2b122673faeb0994125f3e92351fd21596636558',
                    'p1' : $p1.val().trim(),
                    'p2' : $p2.val().trim(),
                };
                
                if (data.p1 == '') {
                    $p1.addClass('is-invalid').focus();
                    return false;
                } else {
                    $p1.removeClass('is-invalid');
                }
                
                if (data.p2 == '') {
                    $p2.addClass('is-invalid').focus();
                    return false;
                } else {
                    $p2.removeClass('is-invalid');
                }
                
                if (data.p1 !== data.p2) {
                    $p1.addClass('is-invalid');
                    $p2.addClass('is-invalid');
                    return;
                } else {
                    $p1.removeClass('is-invalid');
                    $p2.removeClass('is-invalid');
                }
                
                let btn = $(this);
                btn.addLoader(function(){
                    getServiceResponse("/system/security/process/save_remember.php", data, 
                        function(response){
                            window.location = 'https://weareallinnovators.com/';
                        },false, function(error){
                            alert(error);
                            btn.removeLoader();
                        })
                })
                
            });
            
        }, [".form-wrapper"])
    })
</script>
</div>
